4     Q Technology (Group) Company Limited Annual Report 2018
                                                                               Corporate Profile
                                                                                             公司介紹


Q Technology (Group) Company Limited (the “Company”, together             丘 鈦 科 技（ 集 團 ）有 限 公 司（「 本 公
with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is a leading mid-to-high end          司」），連同其附屬公司（「本集團」）為一
camera and fingerprint recognition module manufacturer in the             間中國領先的中高端攝像頭模組及指紋
PRC. The Group is primarily engaged in the design, research and           識別模組製造商。本集團主要從事設計、
development, manufacture and sales of camera modules and                  研 發、製 造 和 銷 售 攝 像 頭 模 組 及 指 紋 識
fingerprint recognition modules, and centred on mid-to-high end           別 模 組，並 以 中 國 品 牌 智 能 手 機 及 平 板
camera and fingerprint recognition module market for Chinese              電腦製造商的中高端攝像頭模組和指紋
branded smart phone and tablet PC manufacturers. The Group                識 別 模 組 市 場 為 主。本 集 團 為 中 國 少 數
is one of the first few manufacturers in the PRC to use chip on           最先於攝像頭模組製造中採用板上芯片
board (“COB”) and chip on flex (“COF”) technologies and molding           封 裝(COB)、薄 膜 覆 晶 封 裝(COF)技 術、
on board (“MOB”) and molding on chip (“MOC”) technologies in              板上塑封(MOB)及芯片塑封（MOC ）技術
the manufacture of camera modules, as well as to produce ultra-           以及能夠批量生產及銷售4,800萬像素及
thin camera modules with resolutions of 48 mega pixels and                以 上 超 薄 攝 像 頭、雙 ╱ 多 攝 像 頭 模 組、
above, dual/multiple camera modules, 3D modules, under-glass              3D模組和屏下指紋識別模組等不同工藝
fingerprint recognition modules and other fingerprint recognition         指 紋 識 別 模 組 的 製 造 商 之 一。目 前，本
modules with various technologies on a mass production scale.             集團產品覆蓋了二百萬像素至四千八百
Currently, the Group’s product mix covers camera modules                  萬 像 素 的 攝 像 頭 模 組、雙 ╱ 多 攝 像 頭 模
ranging from 2 mega pixels to 48 mega pixels, dual/multiple               組、3D模組、指紋識別模組、汽車攝像頭
camera modules, 3D modules, fingerprint recognition modules,              模 組 及 戶 外 運 動 相 機 模 組 等。透 過 不 斷
automotive camera modules and outdoor sports camera modules.              構 建 在 光 電 設 計、計 算 成 像 和 深 度 學 習
The Group is dedicated to become a technology company which               及 系 統 集 成 方 面 之 能 力，本 集 團 致 力 於
provides intelligent vision systematic solutions for intelligent mobile   成長為一家為智能移動終端提供智能視
terminals, through continuously building up capabilities of optical       覺 系 統 化 解 決 方 案 的 科 技 企 業，以 讓 機
design, computing vision, deep learning and system integration,           器看清看懂整個世界。
with an aim to let the machines see clearly and better understand
the world.

FOREIGN EXCHANGE EXPOSURE                                                    匯兌風險

The Group is exposed to currency risk primarily due to certain               由 於 本 集 團 在 銀 行 借 貸、產 品 銷 售 及 原
receivables, payables, cash balances and loans that are                      材料採購等經營活動中產生的部分應收
denominated in foreign currencies other than RMB arising from                款項、應付款項、現金結餘及貸款中以人
the operating activities such as bank borrowings, product sales              民 幣 以 外 的 外 幣 計 值，因 而 產 生 匯 兌 風
and purchase of raw materials. The Group is also exposed to                  險，亦 主 要 在 美 元 及 港 元 與 人 民 幣 的 兌
currency risk from the exchange or translation of USD and Hong               換或折算中產生匯兌風險。本年度內，由
Kong Dollars into RMB. During the Year, the Group recorded                   於本集團的銷售收入仍以人民幣結算為
an exchange loss of approximately RMB44,613,000 as the                       主，而 多 種 生 產 用 原 材 料 及 部 分 生 產 用

B. Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme                                          B. 首次公開發售前購股權計劃

   1.   Purpose of the Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme:                       1.    首次公開發售前購股權計劃的
                                                                                目的：

        The Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme was a share incentive                   首次公開發售前購股權計劃旨
        scheme established to recognise and acknowledge the                     在嘉許及表揚曾經或可能已對
        contributions that the Pre-IPO Eligible Participants (as                本集團作出貢獻的合資格參與
        defined in paragraph 2 below) had made to the Group.                    者（ 定義見下文第2段）。首次公
        The Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme provided the Pre-                       開發售前購股權計劃將向首次
        IPO Eligible Participants with an opportunity to have a                 公開發售前合資格參與者提供
        personal stake in the Company with a view to achieving                  一個於本公司擁有個人權益的
        the following purposes:                                                 機會，以達致下列目標：

        (i)    motivate the Pre-IPO Eligible Participants to                    (i)    激勵首次公開發售前合資格
               optimise their performance efficiency for the benefit                   參與者為本集團的利益而優
               of the Group; and                                                       化其表現效率；及

        (ii)   attract and retain or otherwise maintain relationships           (ii)   吸引及挽留首次公開發售前
               with the Pre-IPO Eligible Participants whose                            合資格參與者或與合資格參
               contributions are or will be beneficial to the                          與者保持持續的業務關係，
               longterm growth of the Group.                                           而該等合資格參與者的貢
                                                                                       獻，乃對或將對本集團的長
                                                                                       遠發展有利。

   2.   Participants of the Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme:                  2.    首次公開發售前購股權計劃參
                                                                                與者：

        The Board might, at its discretion, offer to grant an                   首次公開發售前購股權計劃的
        option to subscribe for such number of new Shares                       合資格參與者為董事會全權認
        as the Board might determine to the full-time, key                      為曾對本公司及╱或我們的任
        employees of the Group who, in the sole opinion of the                  何附屬公司作出貢獻的本集團
        Board, had contributed to the Company and/or any of                     主要全職僱員（「首次公開發售
        the subsidiaries (the “Pre-IPO Eligible Participants”).                 前合資格參與者」）。

Responsibilities                                                          職責

the management of the business affairs and the overall                    務 事 宜 管 理 及 整 體 表 現。董 事 會 設 定 本
performance of the Group. The Board sets the Group’s values               集 團 的 價 值 及 標 準，並 確 保 具 備 必 須 的
and standards and ensures that the requisite financial and                財 務 及 人 力 資 源，使 本 集 團 得 以 實 現 其
human resources support are in place for the Group to achieve             宗 旨 目 標。董 事 會 履 行 的 職 能 包 括 但 不
its objectives. The functions performed by the Board include but          限 於 制 訂 本 集 團 業 務 計 劃 及 策 略、決 定
are not limited to formulating the Group’s business plans and             所有重大財務（ 包括主要的資本支出）及
strategies, deciding all significant financial (including major capital   營運事項，發展、監察及審查本集團的企
expenditure) and operational issues, developing, monitoring               業管治及所有其他根據本公司組織章程
and reviewing the Group’s corporate governance and all other              細則（「組織章程細則」）須經由董事會負
functions reserved to the Board under the Company’s articles              責 的 職 能。董 事 會 轄 下 已 設 立 董 事 委 員
of association (the “Articles of Association”). The Board has             會，並 向 該 等 董 事 委 員 會 轉 授 其 各 自 職
established Board committees and has delegated to these Board             權 範 圍 載 列 之 各 項 責 任。該 等 董 事 會 委
committees various responsibilities as set out in their respective        員各自的職權範圍分別於聯交所及本公
terms of reference which are published on the respective websites         司 網 站 刊 發。董 事 會 可 於 其 認 為 適 當 之
of the Stock Exchange and the Company. The Board may from                 時 不 時 授 予 本 集 團 管 理 人 員 若 干 職 能。
time to time delegate certain functions to management of the              管理人員主要負責執行董事會採用及並
Group if and when considered appropriate. The management                  不時獲指派的業務計劃、策略及政策。
is mainly responsible for the execution of the business plans,
strategies and policies adopted by the Board and assigned to it
from time to time.


The Directors have full access to information of the Group and are        董事可於適當之情況下取閱本集團之資
entitled to seek independent professional advice in appropriate           料 並 有 權 徵 詢 獨 立 專 業 意 見，費 用 由 本
circumstances at the Company’s expense.                                   公司支付。

Composition                                                               組成

The Company is committed to the view that the Board should                本公司致力秉持董事會應包括適當比例
include a balanced composition of executive and independent               的 執 行 董 事 及 獨 立 非 執 行 董 事，致 使 董
non-executive Directors so that there is a strong independent             事會具備高度獨立性以能有效作出獨立
element on the Board, which can effectively exercise independent          判斷。
judgment.

Summary of Board Diversity Policy                                    董事會多元化政策摘要

A.   Purpose                                                         A.   目的
     To ensure that the Board has a balance of skills,                    確保董事會具備適用於本公司業務
     experience and diversity of perspectives appropriate to the          要 求 的 技 巧、經 驗 及 思 維 多 元 化 之
     requirements of the Company’s business.                              平衡配套。

B.   Nomination and appointment                                      B.   提名與委任
     The Nomination Committee will identify eligible candidates           提名委員在以用人唯才為原則，以日
     on merit basis based on the Company’s daily business                 常 的 業 務 需 求 為 基 準，並 在 考 慮 董
     needs while taking into account the benefit of diversity of          事 會 成 員 多 元 化 的 裨 益 的 基 礎 上，
     the members of the Board.                                            物色合資格的候選人。

C.   Measurable objectives                                           C.   可計量的目標
     Selection of candidates to the Board will be based on a              甄選董事會人選將按一系列多元化
     range of diversity, including but not limited to gender, age,        範疇為基準，包括但不限於性別、年
     language, cultural background, educational background,               齡、語 言、文 化 背 景、教 育 背 景、行
     industry experience and professional experience.                     業經驗及專業經驗。

D.   Monitoring and reporting                                        D.   監察及彙報
     The Nomination Committee will disclose the composition of            提 名 委 員 會 將 每 年 在 本 公 司《企 業
     the Board in the Company’s Corporate Governance Report               管治報告》中披露董事會組成，並監
     each year and monitor the implementation of this policy.             察本政策的執行。

E.   Reviewing this policy                                           E.   檢討本政策
     The Nomination Committee will review this policy in                  提名委員會將在適當時候檢討本政
     due course to ensure that this policy is effective. The              策，以確保本政策行之有效。提名委
     Nomination Committee will review this policy and the                 員會將至少每年，或在適當時候，檢
     measurable objectives at least annually, or as appropriate,          討 本 政 策 與 可 計 量 目 標，以 確 保 董
     to ensure the continued effectiveness of the Board.                  事會持續行之有效。

Director Nomination Policy                                              董事提名政策

The Company has developed and adopted a nomination policy               本公司已制定並採納董事提名政策（「提
for directors (the “Nomination Policy”), under which appointment        名政策」），依據提名政策的規定經審慎
of new Directors or re-election of Directors have been carefully        考慮並按正式程序委任新董事或重選董
considered as required by the Nomination Policy and based on            事。本公司提名政策之摘要列載如下：
formal procedures. A summary of the Nomination Policy of the
Company is set out below:


A.   Purpose                                                            A.   目的
     The Nomination Policy is aimed to set out the procedures and            提名政策旨在列明提名本公司董事
     criteria for nominating director candidates of the Company              候 選 人 之 程 序 及 標 準，以 確 保 董 事
     to ensure that all nominations for the Board members are fair           會 成 員 的 所 有 提 名 屬 公 平 及 透 明，
     and transparent, thereby facilitating the efficient and balanced        從而促進董事會高效平衡發展。
     development of the Board.


B.   Nomination procedures                                              B.   提名程序
     1.   The Secretary of the Nominating Committee collects                 1.    提名委員會秘書收集及分析候
          and analyses the necessary personal data submitted by                    選人所提交的必要的個人資料。
          candidates.


     2.   The Chairman of the Nomination Committee will convene              2.    提名委員會主席將召開提名委
          a meeting of the Nomination Committee or circulate a                     員會會議或向提名委員會成員
          resolution in writing to the members of the Nomination                   傳閱書面決議案，以根據職權範
          Committee to consider matters in accordance with its                     圍考慮有關事項。
          terms of reference.


     3.   For filling a casual vacancy to the Board or as an                 3.    填補董事會的臨時空缺或為董
          addition to the Board, the Nomination Committee will                     事會增補董事時，提名委員會將
          conduct the relevant selection process (coupled with                     對獲提名的候選人執行相關甄
          the relevant selection criteria) against the nominated                   選 程 序（ 連 同 相 關 甄 選 標 準），
          candidate and make recommendations to the Board for                      並向董事會提出推薦意見以供
          consideration, and the Board will then make a decision                   考慮，而董事會屆時將決定獲提
          as to whether the nominated candidate shall be eligible                  名的候選人是否有資格獲委任
          to be appointed as a director of the Company.                            為本公司董事。

 CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                主席及行政總裁

 The roles of the Chairman and the Chief Executive Officer are       主席及行政總裁負責不同任務及由不同
 separate and performed by different individuals to ensure a         人士擔當，以確保權力及授權之平衡，使
 balance of power and authority so that power is not concentrated    任 何 單 一 人 士 不 能 包 攬 全 部 權 力。主 席
 in any one individual. Mr. He Ningning, Chairman, is responsible    何 寧 寧 先 生 負 責 董 事 會 管 理，而 行 政 總
 for the management of the Board while Mr. Wang Jianqiang,           裁王健強先生負責管理本集團業務及整
 Chief Executive Officer, is responsible for managing the Group’s    體 運 作。主 席 與 行 政 總 裁 各 自 之 責 任 顯
 business and overall operations. There is a clear division of       然有別。
 responsibilities between Chairman and Chief Executive Officer.

4   Environmental                                                  4 環境

    4.1 Environmental-related Policies                              4.1 環境相關政策

        –   As a leading camera and fingerprint recognition           － 作為一家領先的中國攝像頭
            module manufacturer in the PRC, apart from                  模組和指紋識別模組製造
            attaching importance to scientific research                 商，在 重 視 科 研 創 新、高 效
            innovation, high-efficient production and lean              生產和精細管理外，董事會
            management, the Board believes that it is crucial to        相信切實做好環保節能，改
            business operation to effectively conduct efforts on        善生產耗用資源的效率，以
            environmental conservation and improve resources            及盡力降低生產過程中對附
            consumption efficiency of production, and strives to        近土地、水源以及其他天然
            reduce the hazards affecting the surrounding lands,         資源做成的災害，是業務營
            water sources and other natural resources during            運的重中之重。
            production.


        –   The Group is well recognized in the construction          － 本集團在環境生產系統的
            of its environmental production system and has              構建上備受認同，已取得了
            obtained ISO14001 (Environmental Management)                ISO14001（ 環境管理）的資
            Qualification Certificate.                                  格證明。

        –   The Group complies with the state and regional            － 本集團遵守國家和地區性的
            relevant environmental protection and prevention            相關環保防治條例，積極配
            rules. It also actively cooperates with government          合政府部門例行性、專項性
            departments in inspections on safety, environmental         及突擊性對企業進行的安
            protection, firefighting and occupational health of         全、環 保、消 防、職 業 健 康

            unexpected manner, and make filing records for              環境影響進行登記表備案，
            the environmental impact of construction projects           並依例邀請第三方機構編制
            as well as invites third party agencies to prepare          環評報告等。
            environment impact assessment report according to
            the regulations.


        –   In accordance with the regulations, the Group             － 按 照 規 定，本 集 團 由 二 零
            began to disclose the five environmental key                一七年的報告開始披露五

 Basis for opinion                                                    意見的基礎

 We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards        我們已根據香港會計師公會（「香港會計
 on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of           師公會」）頒佈的《香港審計準則》（「香
 Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”). Our responsibilities        港 審 計 準 則」）進 行 審 計。我 們 就 該 等 準
 under those standards are further described in the Auditor’s         則 項 下 承 擔 的 責 任 已 在 本 報 告「核 數 師
 responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated financial         就 審 計 合 併 財 務 報 表 須 承 擔 的 責 任」一
 statements section of our report. We are independent of the Group    節 中 詳 述。根 據 香 港 會 計 師 公 會 頒 佈 的
 in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional      《專業會計師道德守則》（「守則」）及開
 Accountants (“the Code”) together with any ethical requirements      曼群島中任何與我們審計合併財務報表
 that are relevant to our audit of the consolidated financial         有關的道德要求，我們獨立於 貴集團，
 statements in the Cayman Islands, and we have fulfilled our other    並已遵循該等道德要求及守則中的其他
 ethical responsibilities in accordance with these requirements and   專業道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的
 the Code. We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is     審計憑證是充足和適當地為我們的意見
 sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.       提供基礎。

 Key audit matters                                                    關鍵審計事項

 Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional        關鍵審計事項是指根據我們的專業判
 judgement, were of most significance in our audit of the             斷，認 為 對 本 期 合 併 財 務 報 表 的 審 計 最

 rare circumstances, we determine that a matter should not be               們報告中溝通某事項造成的負面後果超
 communicated in our report because the adverse consequences                過 產 生 的 公 眾 利 益，我 們 決 定 不 應 在 報
 of doing so would reasonably be expected to outweigh the public            告中溝通該事項。
 interest benefits of such communication.

 1    Significant accounting policies                                    1 主要會計政策

      (a) Statement of compliance                                         (a) 合規聲明

          These financial statements have been prepared in                  該等財務報表乃按照所有適用
          accordance with all applicable International Financial            國際財務報告準則（「國際財務
          Reporting Standards (“IFRSs”), which collective term              報 告 準 則」）而 編 製。國 際 財 務
          includes all applicable individual IFRSs, International           報告準則包括由國際會計準則
          Accounting Standards (“IASs”) and Interpretations issued          委 員 會（「 國 際 會 計 準 則 委 員
          by the International Accounting Standards Board (the              會」）頒 佈 的 所 有 適 用 的 個 別 國
          “IASB”), accounting principles generally accepted in              際財務報告準則、國際會計準則
          Hong Kong and the disclosure requirements of the Hong            （「國 際 會 計 準 則」）及 詮 釋、香
          Kong Companies Ordinance. These financial statements              港公認會計原則以及香港公司
          also comply with the applicable disclosure provisions             條例的披露規定。該等財務報表
          of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The           亦符合香港聯合交易所有限公
          Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited. Significant                  司證券上市規則的適用披露規
          accounting policies adopted by the Group are disclosed            定。本集團採納的主要會計政策
          below.                                                            披露如下。

          The IASB has issued certain new and revised IFRSs                 國際會計準則委員會已頒佈若
          that are first effective or available for early adoption for      干新訂及經修訂國際財務報告

                    a financial asset and therefore recognises                         損」模 式。預 期 信 貸 虧
                    ECLs earlier than under the “incurred loss”                        損模式要求持續計量

                      asset.                                                                 損撥備）計算。

                      At each reporting date, the Group assesses                             於各報告日期，本集團
                      whether a financial asset is credit-impaired. A                        評估金融資產是否出現

                      asset have occurred.                                                   響的事件時，金融資產
                                                                                             出現信貸減值。

                      The recoverable amount of an asset is the                           資產的可收回金額為其
                      greater of its fair value less costs of disposal                    公平值減銷售成本與使
                      and value in use. In assessing value in use, the                    用價值中的較高者。評

                      their present value using a pre-tax discount                        來現金流量乃利用反映
                      rate that reflects current market assessments                       資金時間價值的現時市
                      of the time value of money and the risks                            場評估及資產的特定風
                      specific to the asset. Where an asset does                          險的除稅前貼現率貼現
                      not generate cash inflows largely independent                       至其現值。倘資產所產
                      of those from other assets, the recoverable                         生的現金流入未能大致
                      amount is determined for the smallest                               獨立於其他資產所產生
                      Group of assets that generates cash inflows                         的現金流入，則釐定可

          present value of the expenditure expected to settle the               清償責任所需開支的現值列賬。
          obligation.


          Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                  如需要經濟利益流出的可能性
          benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                    不大，或是無法對有關金額作出
          estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                  可靠估計，便會將該責任披露為
          contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow               或然負債，但經濟利益流出的可
          of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations,                 能性極低的情況除外。如本集團
          whose existence will only be confirmed by the                         的責任須視乎一宗或多宗未來
          occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future                    事件是否發生才能確定是否存
          events are also disclosed as contingent liabilities unless            在，則亦會披露為或然負債，但
          the probability of outflow of economic benefits is remote.            經濟利益流出的可能性極低的
                                                                                情況除外。

      authorities whereby the PRC subsidiaries are required            一一年十二月一日開始，中國附屬公司須
      to make contributions at the rate of 20% starting from 1         以 合 資 格 僱 員 薪 金 的20%（ 二 零 一 一 年
      December 2011 (before 1 December 2011: 18%) of the               十二月一日前：18% ）的比率向該計劃作
      eligible employees’ salary rate to the Schemes. The Group        出供款。本集團已積累所需供款，於供款
      has accrued for the required contributions which are remitted    到期時匯付予各地方政府機關。地方政府
      to the respective local government authorities when the          機關就應向該計劃所涉及的退休僱員支

      are responsible for the pension obligations payable to the

   Kong Employment Ordinance and not previously covered             計劃（「強積金計劃」）。強積金計劃為由

   is a defined contribution retirement plan administered by        根據強積金計劃，僱主及其僱員須分別向

   subject to a cap of monthly relevant income of HK$30,000         出的供款即時歸屬。
   (HK$25,000 prior to June 2014). Contributions to the plan
   vest immediately.

 29 Financial risk management and fair values                            29 金融風險管理及公平值

      Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency           本 集 團 於 日 常 業 務 過 程 中 產 生 信 貸、流
      risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.          動資金、利率及貨幣風險。下文說明本集
      The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk          團面臨該等風險的狀況及本集團管理該
      management policies and practices used by the Group to              等風險所採用的金融風險管理政策及慣
      manage these risks are described below.                             例。


      (a) Credit risk                                                     (a) 信貸風險

           Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will             信貸風險指交易對手未能履行其合
           default on its contractual obligations resulting in a               約義務而使本集團產生財務虧損的
           financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is             風 險。本 集 團 的 信 貸 風 險 主 要 歸 因
           primarily attributable to trade and other receivables. The          於 貿 易 及 其 他 應 收 款 項。本 集 團 因
           Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash and               現金及現金等價物及衍生金融資產
           cash equivalents and derivative financial assets is limited         而 產 生 的 信 貸 風 險 有 限，因 為 交 易
           because the counterparties are banks, for which the                 對手為本集團認為信貸風險較低的
           Group considers to have low credit risk.                            銀行。


           The Group does not provide financial guarantee which                本 集 團 並 無 提 供 財 務 擔 保，令 本 集
           would expose the Group to credit risk.                              團面臨信貸風險。


          Trade and other receivables                                          貿易及其他應收款項

           For the year ended 31 December 2018, certain amounts                截至二零一八年十二月三十一日止
           of sales were related directly to the related parties (Note         年 度，若 干 銷 售 款 項 與 直 接 向 關 聯
           31(b)).                                                             方的銷售有關（ 附註31(b) ）。


           The management considers that related parties are                   管理層認為關聯方乃受本集團的控
           under influence of the Group’s Controlling Shareholder,             股 股 東 影 響，故 向 關 聯 公 司 的 銷 售
           hence no material credit risk exists on sales to related            並不存在重大信貸風險。
           companies.

          segments, the loss allowance based on past due status             按逾期狀態計算的虧損撥備沒有在

          customer bases.

          endorsement. Bill holders in due course preserve right            據。倘票據遭拒付，則票據持有人適

    judgements

      are based on experience and other factors, including               因應當時情況認為屬對未來事件的合理
      expectations of future events that are believed to be              預期）為基準持續評估。
      reasonable under the circumstances.


      The selection of critical accounting policies, the judgements      重要會計政策的選擇、影響該等政策應用
      and other uncertainties affecting application of those policies    的判斷及其他不明朗因素以及所呈報業
      and the sensitivity of reported results to changes in condition    績對條件及假設變動的敏感度均為審閱
      and assumptions are factors to be considered when reviewing        此等財務報表時所考慮的因素。主要會計
      these financial statements. The principal accounting policies      政策載列於附註1。本集團相信，以下重
      are set forth in note 1. The Group believes the following          要會計政策涉及編製此等財務報表所使

      judgements and estimates used in the preparation of these
      financial statements.


      (a) Loss allowance for trade receivables                           (a) 貿易應收款項的虧損撥備

           The Group estimates the loss allowances for trade                本集團透過評估預期信貸虧損估計
           receivables by assessing the ECLs. This requires the             貿 易 應 收 款 項 的 虧 損 撥 備。這 需 要
           use of estimates and judgements. ECLs are based on               運 用 估 計 及 判 斷。預 期 信 貸 虧 損 乃
           the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted          根據本集團的過往信貸虧損經驗，
           for factors that are specific to the debtors and an              就具體債務人的因素作出之調整及

   interest expense accrued on the outstanding balance of the             負債未償還結餘所產生的利息開支及使

   instead of the current policy of recognising rental expenses           內按系統基準確認根據經營租約所產生
   incurred under operating leases on a systematic basis over             的租賃開支。作為一項可行權宜方法，承
   the lease term. As a practical expedient, the lessee can elect         租人可選擇不將此會計模式應用於短期
   not to apply this accounting model to short-term leases (i.e.          租賃（ 即租期為12個月或以下）及低價值
